engine</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dc-9c87-caf278f2fd07" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Contradiction engine (03_IMMUNE)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806c-8d87-f11b15603eaf" class="">E) Simulation engine</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b8-8c7c-f4bbaf3934ae" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">multi-loop coupled dynamics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8040-969b-e35a3902c89e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">regime map (birth→expansion→dominance→decay)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80db-8c24-f51bf19abe20" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">perturbation + control delay stress tests</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-bdd0-f5779d160db9" class="">F) Cross-scale + cross-species</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8032-a318-d0f31f63d153" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">transfer operator</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806f-8aef-e83ba02910c6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">fidelity metrics</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-ba8f-fd0a16155b18" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">bridge consistency tests</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-980c-dbf97b02ed08"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-9b3c-faa5a2524251" class="">9.8 “Close all gaps” checklist (mechanical, final)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800e-bd5c-fd269dc16cf6" class="">A system is “gap-closed” iff:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a3-a267-e7701b2b70e6" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Symbol closure</strong>: no undeclared symbols.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f1-a170-ed4308c2d5a6" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Operator closure</strong>: every operator has tests + gates.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-a52e-e663e06961c0" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Gate closure</strong>: every load-bearing claim binds to gates that PASS.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8002-9830-dc069311ce55" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><strong>Access closure</strong>: ownership projector exists for every evidence channel.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-b5b7-fceed907fbd1" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><strong>Scale closure</strong>: micro↔macro bridge error bounded.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8080-be42-f49bacc2bef7" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Latent closure</strong>: intangible never load-bearing unless admissible.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ad-b8f0-e34c433dde38" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><strong>Termination closure</strong>: Valid/Bounded/Invalid forced by rule set.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c2-aee5-f8e84f467389"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80da-ba7b-f21b0c3f019f" class="">9.9 Next deliverable (to proceed “MORE” without expanding scope infinitely)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-b348-d22e9b021b1e" class="">Choose one output to materialize next (each is deterministic and SSOT-friendly):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f4-a5e8-c9ef4612ff44" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><code><strong>symbol_table.json</strong></code><strong> (complete)</strong> — all variables, units, domains, scale, channel, ownership.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fd-bbfd-e9809b949978" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><code><strong>gate_catalog.json</strong></code><strong> (complete)</strong> — every gate equation + thresholds + failure modes.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e6-9243-ce0616df85ac" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><code><strong>loop_library.json</strong></code><strong> (200 loops)</strong> — generated loops across domains with required gates.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803c-b277-c58060edf894" class="">If you want “MORE” in the strict sense, the correct next step is <strong>(2) gate_catalog.json</strong>, because it closes the most gaps fastest.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8081-beea-e7fd9d833db4" class="">9) EXTENSION — Exhaustive “MORE” as a closed catalog: symbols, operators, gates, loops, and generators</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801c-8682-ffe6fee6bfd5" class="">This is the missing layer: <strong>equations that generate equations</strong>, plus <strong>closure operators</strong> that prevent silent gaps across micro↔macro, time↔space, biology↔civilization, and observable↔latent.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8016-9391-ec2b35af236b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8094-bdd9-c034473d0dda" class="">9.1 Symbol universe (SSOT) — Everything must reduce to these base objects</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8017-aba9-c2d505430fd3" class="">Base sets</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800f-b096-c0264b3532d5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Time index:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8033-8544-ca0828aac6da" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Scale index: (micro / meso / macro)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-aae6-f78b26d66dd2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Species index:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8099-b569-eea4b0f7e3d3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Channel index: (S_bio, S_env, S_soc, S_mach, S_hist, S_logic, S_lat)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e5-9df2-e4e264602c6a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Ownership index:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a0-a805-cfb3f7ed9bbf" class="">State (minimal closed core)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8001-bd58-ddfea113a040" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_t = (q_t,G_t,U_t,\Xi_t,R_t,D_t,P_t,M_t,\tau_t,C^{acc}_t,\Pi_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807e-a1c9-ddaaecff98fd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: constraint density (boundary/structure constraints per volume)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e8-9197-d9c001dfe24a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: usable gradient (free energy / predictive contrast)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804b-a8eb-fc6ce23ddc37" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: unused write capacity (fresh DOF available for durable records)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804a-a2ba-d6b67520f588" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: effective noise (overwrite + corruption pressure)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806e-8ccf-c5cb98c9134e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: record redundancy (durable copies count / strength)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bd-b621-dd0b5065fba4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: recursion depth (stable stacked models count)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a4-b279-f4e14e8bcef2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: available power for maintenance/repair</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f7-ba15-ea13d146d4d9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: usable memory capacity (bits)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-aa52-f8e1a1e64170" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: effective control delay</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8084-b1ad-ed4fcbe2eaa9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: accessible channel capacity (bits/s)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8072-8799-eaa18017edb8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: access projector (ownership/permission)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8039-8738-cff44bff8a7b" class="">Everything else is a derived field and must be declared in the symbol table.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8005-8e95-fff5260abf00"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802d-9c21-c60611e9fc20" class="">9.2 Operator algebra (equations that generate equations)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801f-b724-c8916394b6c7" class="">9.2.1 The operator-of-operators: “closure compiler”</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8049-956f-cf14ad5c9efb" class="">Define the closure compiler:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804d-a303-f287b7d7b6ba" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{C}\_{close}:\ (\mathcal{V},\mathcal{O},\mathcal{G}) \mapsto (\mathcal{E},\mathcal{G}&#x27;)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8047-bc62-d473be66f382" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: operators declared</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-a2ef-e42bb4134e8f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: gates declared</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800c-9805-e6c8ed6cf592" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: generated equations (complete set)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-a726-ce519259802e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: augmented gates required by those equations</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fe-8737-e611e955d8e4" class=""><strong>Invariant</strong>: no operator may be introduced without emitting its required gates.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e3-94b3-e83c5c96611b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall O\in\mathcal{O},\ \exists\,\mathrm{ReqGates}(O)\subseteq\mathcal{G}&#x27;</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805f-ad71-cd59cd10f2f8" class="">9.2.2 Minimal operator types (exhaustive)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-a970-e57bb0d35038" class="">Every transformation must be one of these operator classes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8031-84d6-df7efbb038e2" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Update</strong> (state evolution)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-abd7-c8444b3644b6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1}=F(x_t,u_t,s_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806b-af32-ca391c399305" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Projection</strong> (scale/species mapping)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-be93-e4e96049be77" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
X_t=\mathcal{R}_{s\to s&#x27;}(x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-9fbf-f00a382fb048" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Fusion</strong> (multi-sense integration)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fa-94fa-c1ee03970e0d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat x=\arg\min_x \sum_i w_i\|\Pi_i(A_ix-y_i)\|^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8060-a978-e0ab7b0171c0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Compression</strong> (record formation)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ee-b12b-f31a9365c017" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L_t = \mathrm{len}(\mathrm{Compress}(C(x_{0:t})))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804b-a604-d6a0e26976b8" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Coding</strong> (error-correcting stabilization)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c2-8284-e1bac3636c60" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p_e(\Xi_t,r_t) &lt; p_{th}(r_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ca-a029-cd5eafe91ec7" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Audit/Proof</strong> (structural validity)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-858a-fad45ad91835" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Closure}(\text{claims},\text{symbols},\text{gates}) \in \{\mathrm{Complete},\mathrm{Incomplete}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8058-a68c-d3ecde186c6a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Ownership</strong> (access control)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8089-a0fc-d4432cc39a3f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y_i^{acc}=\Pi_i y_i,\quad \Pi_i\in[0,1]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fa-8c6e-dc6ba54e1333"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-914d-d8e7a93b4502" class="">9.3 Gate catalog (the missing “hard walls”)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8054-8433-c8e693a12ca8" class="">These are the gates you must add to reach “exhaustive”:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-9919-d81efb1a9513" class="">G1 — SSOT Gate</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8088-8c92-d64ca3d4f4cd" class="">No duplicate authority modules in import graph.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8009-b6d3-c26483731ea5" class="">G2 — Determinism Gate</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-812d-e875fdf1b906" class="">No randomness, no time-dependent logic paths.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-a826-d30ff50d210a" class="">G3 — Arrow Gate</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8097-b260-d5d5f145325c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\beta G_t &gt; \kappa \Xi_t R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8030-b3dd-f1e3213e45c6" class="">G4 — Code Gate</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8047-94cc-c423d9824a41" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p_e(\Xi_t,r_t) &lt; p_{th}(r_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8007-956b-dad05f6889ac" class="">G5 — Control Gate (delay stability)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800f-adb7-d325f8677983" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau_t &lt; \tau_{max}(D_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a5-9fe6-f2c25119fda3" class="">G6 — Budget Gate (Landauer floor)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808f-9ffa-d7d335c8b800" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_t \ge kT\ln 2 \cdot \dot B(D_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808f-86fc-d76aafe3de8d" class="">G7 — Memory Gate (finite store)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8027-930f-e5bb1a867b17" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{records}(R_t)+I_{models}(D_t) \le M_t \le I_{max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a9-a827-ca5d941bff96" class="">G8 — Identifiability Gate (cause vs correlation)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-9905-d67ba01c60d6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{rank}(A_{acc}) \ge \dim(x_{claimed})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ee-82c8-d1542e442e34" class="">G9 — Ownership Gate (all information has owners)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809c-8338-ff6627e6b2d2" class="">Load-bearing claims require:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8089-ad96-d60bbc05d66e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pi_{support} \ge \Pi_{min}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-90ef-e8dbf17d230e" class="">G10 — Latent Admissibility Gate (intangible handling)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-88b8-d34d912c748e" class="">Latent channel cannot be load-bearing unless:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-b12e-eede069514a1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pi_{lat}&gt;0\ \wedge\ D_{lat}\ge\theta_{lat}\ \wedge\ \neg \mathrm{Contradict}(z,y_{obs})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f8-9d11-f557d320cb3f" class="">G11 — Scale Closure Gate (micro↔macro consistency)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8025-a35e-f2f881e4b49f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\|\mathcal{R}(F(x_t)) - F_M(\mathcal{R}(x_t))\|\le \epsilon_{scale}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808a-bc08-ddcb54941ff9" class="">G12 — Cross-species Transfer Gate</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808c-85a9-e3a0a30d3541" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Fid}(\theta^\sigma,\mathcal{T}_{\sigma&#x27;\to\sigma}(\theta^{\sigma&#x27;}))\ge\theta_{min}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805d-9a0e-f40c31b7a98f" class="">G13 — Horizon/Accessibility Gate (physics-limited access)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a9-8003-cfb2c95d8429" class="">Accessible information is capped:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e5-b528-e7e23673bf82" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I_{acc}(t)\le I_{max}(H_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f9-9d1d-cb6a892b1ee1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8049-929e-c9ef4af9727f" class="">9.4 The loop generator (produces “all loops” as one matrix)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-9d11-fd1cf0b653a2" class="">Define a loop template:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f3-afe6-c03426718891" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L = (\text{StateVars},\ \text{UpdateOps},\ \text{Gates},\ \text{Outputs})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f0-bd0f-c769d240c47a" class="">A loop is “valid” iff all its gates are declared and testable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-9080-c189a0126081" class="">Loop kernel matrix (single unified form)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8083-938f-c4ea2db64dec" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbf{L}_t=
\begin{bmatrix}
q_t &amp; G_t &amp; U_t &amp; \Xi_t\\
R_t &amp; D_t &amp; P_t &amp; M_t\\
\tau_t &amp; C^{acc}_t &amp; \Pi_t &amp; \text{stage}_t
\end{bmatrix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808f-9c1e-c0fc390a5ebb" class="">Update law:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c2-bab6-ece69c9ad660" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbf{L}_{t+1}=F(\mathbf{L}_t;\ u_t,s_t)\quad\text{s.t.}\quad \bigwedge_k \mathrm{Gate}_k(\mathbf{L}_t)=\mathrm{PASS}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801e-9c27-d6c928c92acd"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fd-bf0c-e1a5edd95369" class="">9.5 Missing “intangible” layer formalization (without breaking closure)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8013-8784-c838b0856f4b" class="">You want “energy/information before birth and after death” and “spiritual patterns” as usable invariants.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-bc3d-dd807761fb40" class="">AMOS can include this <strong>only</strong> via explicit support typing:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-9ff0-da7a261af6b5" class="">Support types</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Primitive</strong>, or</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8036-b789-ff23af3ee3ca" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cc-8908-c468b63b8faa"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805e-a6df-fc4b36638ecb" class="">9.6 “Equations that generate equations” \mathcal{G}&#x27;=\mathcal{G}\cup \bigcup_{e\in\mathcal{E}} \mathrm{ReqGates}(e)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809f-a6c3-f0d7b26b7762" class="">9.6.2 Symbol closure operator</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808a-856a-da15288a2023" class="">Given equations, generate symbol table:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8008-afeb-d365b75a6bf2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{V}&#x27;=\mathcal{V}\cup \bigcup_{e\in\mathcal{E}} \mathrm{Symbols}(e)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8087-88ea-c26dbe63d011" class="">9.6.3 Claim compiler</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809c-8f47-e4be0ce96e09" class="">Given a narrative, produce typed claims:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a3-9adf-cea38e1a1bee" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802e-8a25-fcd635e36c90" class="">A system is “gap-closed” iff:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dc-89b4-e1db2b562eae" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Symbol closure</strong>: no undeclared symbols.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8039-9cba-de29903f8aca" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Operator closure</strong>: every operator has tests + gates.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e1-affc-c355cb8a218b" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Gate closure</strong>: every load-bearing claim binds to gates that start="5"><li><strong>Scale closure</strong>: micro↔macro bridge error bounded.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-804b-87f0-e4432141a035" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><strong>Latent closure</strong>: intangible never load-bearing unless admissible.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8032-b7c3-f21b8d079268" class="numbered-list" start="7"><li><strong>Termination closure</strong>: Valid/Bounded/Invalid forced by rule set.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8077-bbd1-f19ab4adae5a" class="">2) EQUATIONS++ — Fully parameterized forms + deterministic toy instantiations</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80be-9ed0-ffe8983eac2b" class="">2.1 Parameterized subsystem equations (canonical forms)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a2-8d3e-f07f3fe493de" class=""><strong>(A) Biology (minimal coupled control-friendly form)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80fc-8952-d17189e286dd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
N_{t+1}=N_t + a_N(\mathrm{stim}_t) - b_N(N_t-N^\*) + \epsilon^N_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80bf-bc2f-c3bf24d5f112" class="">H_{t+1}=H_t + a_H(N_t) - b_H(H_t-H^*) + \epsilon^H_t<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80ff-be76-dc6ffc55f3e6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I^m_{t+1}=I^m_t + a_I(\Xi_t,H_t) - b_I(I^m_t-I^{m\*}) + \epsilon^I_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-802c-a4e5-f8f789fddd41" class="">M_{t+1}=M_t + a_M(\mathrm{intake}_t,G_t) - b_M(M_t-M^*) + \epsilon^M_t<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8086-a46a-c768389dab20" class=""><strong>(B) Records + overwrite + threshold collapse</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8012-9ef2-cb3532553eeb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{t+1}=R_t+\beta G_t-\kappa \Xi_t R_t-\lambda\,\mathbf{1}[p(\Xi_t)\ge p_{th}(r_t)]R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a6-82d4-deac6fb1d7e5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p(\Xi_t)=1-\exp(-c_\Xi\Xi_t)\quad,\quad p_{th}(r)=\sigma(\alpha(r-r^\*))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8007-bd12-f4ee78bef4d5" class=""><strong>(C) Write-capacity</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80e0-8148-caa276a3dbae" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_{t+1}=U_t-\gamma\max(0,R_{t+1}-R_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8098-915e-d0c65b519f8f" class=""><strong>(D) Recursion depth (error + delayed repair)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8018-89c6-de2896feaf4e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\varepsilon^{(d)}_{t+1}=\alpha_d\varepsilon^{(d)}_t+\eta_d(t)-r_d(t)-\rho_d\,p_d(t-\tau_d)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8074-98c6-eaa0b12f7c5b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
r_d(t)=\min\{r^{max}_d,\ \chi_d\,G_t\}-\zeta_d\Xi_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-b19c-c9ec74736133" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_{t+1}=D_t + \mathbf{1}\big[\forall d\le D_t: \varepsilon^{(d)}_{t+1}\le \epsilon_d\big] - \mathbf{1}[\exists d: \varepsilon^{(d)}_{t+1}&gt;\epsilon_d]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807d-be9d-fda17d40143d" class=""><strong>(E) Control stability (discrete-time)</strong><br/>Linearization:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803b-9879-fcad7cc916f8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1}\approx A_tx_t+B_tu_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-beea-c1d1ce62c9ff" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
u_t=-K_tx_t,\quad \rho(A_t-B_tK_t)&lt;1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8078-8f67-fad7cdfab8b0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
A_t \leftarrow A_t + \Delta(\tau_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-9e0c-e5d3df706256" class=""><strong>(F) EM/network capacity (machine-accessible)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800f-8f49-fe1ac9bf3729" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_t=B_t\log_2(1+\mathrm{SNR}_t),\quad C^{acc}_t=\Pi_t\cdot C_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8014-af86-d8d9dc82cdb3" class=""><strong>(G) Ownership projector (operator form)</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b1-b5e0-dadf1804790d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pi_t=\mathsf{P}_{owner}(\mathrm{agent},\mathrm{context},\mathcal{O})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803e-9101-fd047eec1232" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pi_t=0\ \nRightarrow\ \text{nonexistence}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-808e-8699-e73b8dd755d1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c3-9c10-e715afcafe94" class="">2.2 Deterministic toy instantiations (no randomness)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-9928-eb14116dce08" class="">Use bounded, piecewise-linear functions so outputs are reproducible.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30cc5e6f-95bd-800a-b1b8-c035f0972ad6" class="">Example:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8071-92f5-e6d906ccac90" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8064-9fed-cb7be822d336" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8091-a536-c90493cccaef" to:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8013-8618-f998060a275a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">generate saturation regimes,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8051-93d3-c29586301765" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">create threshold collapses,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805a-a08a-d6426513c4ff" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">prove gate separation mechanically.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8083-bd5f-cbd4c7c429cb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 \mathcal{L}_i=\langle X_i,\ \theta_i,\ F_i,\ G_i,\ \mathcal{R}_i,\ \Pi_i,\ \mathrm{stage}_i \rangle</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul loops = product size; you can generate “next 200” without hand-writing.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8082-938b-ffe05bb48d77" class="">3.3 Canonical style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b4-a784-c4794c869623" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{t+1}=R_t+\beta G_t-\kappa \Xi_t R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8066-8bdd-f15b9fddc67a" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Write-Budget Loop</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-806c-b71d-e54bfc97c3e0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_{t+1}=U_t-\gamma(R_{t+1}-R_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-803f-84ea-d94259de3abc" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Delay-Instability Loop</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-807a-8419-f45e731ecf68" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\rho(A_t-B_tK_t)&lt;1,\quad A_t\leftarrow A_t+\Delta(\tau_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c4-ae0e-c1951de350d2" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Repair Budget Loop</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8056-bfc8-e28ca69ecf53" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
r_d(t)=\min\{r^{max}_d,\chi_dG_t\}-\zeta_d\Xi_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cd-9b32-fa02daa8e176" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Depth Growth Loop</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b0-85fb-c00e5dc2703d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_{t+1}=D_t+\mathbf{1}[\varepsilon^{(d)}\le \epsilon_d\ \forall d\le D_t]-\mathbf{1}[\exists d:\varepsilon^{(d)}&gt;\epsilon_d]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8084-84fc-c64f353a5e10" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Institution Rewrite Loop</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8084-8852-faedadd396d1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{L}_{t+1}=\mathrm{Rewrite}(\mathcal{L}_t,\mathcal{K}_t,\pi_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c1-8a9d-d051c2364dd4" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Knowledge Loss Loop</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8059-9009-e500b8c4e25a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{K}_{t+1}=\mathcal{K}_t+\Delta R_t-\mathrm{loss}(\Xi_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809c-b67e-e3b6b47234fb" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Cross-Species Fidelity Loop</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80b6-a3e4-de8142a6e98a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Fid}(\mathcal{L}^{child},\mathcal{L}^{parent})\ge \theta_L</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a5-a8a2-ca23eff8504b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Ownership/Access Loop</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8070-9974-e49c5b3ef5c7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C^{acc}_t=\Pi_t\cdot B_t\log_2(1+\mathrm{SNR}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80f3-b4d7-e8948efe19f0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Biological Stability Loop</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-809b-b81c-f90e8b85dcc1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\|M_t-M^\*\|\le \delta_M</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80a1-9ecb-f8d50f0a6b49" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Noise Escalation Loop (Dominance→Decay)</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80d9-80eb-d0d7cebf2b1a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Xi_{t+1}=\Xi_t+\delta_\Xi \mathbf{1}[s_t\in Dc,De]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-801e-ad65-cb4d041e5ef7" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Latent Bounded Loop</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30cc5e6f-95bd-805f-9d61-df302686ea3f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Z_{t+1}=h(Z_t)+\nu_t,\quad \text{non-load-bearing unless }D_{lat}\ge\theta_{lat}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80cb-8a72-fc0a1e88bf8a"/></div><div stages)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80dd-98ae-dfcd58fbc045" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>loop_matrix.json</code> style="list-style-type:disc"><code>loop_stage_map.json</code> (Birth/Expansion/Dominance/Decay placement)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8048-8373-ee2214df99f7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>loop_tests.json</code> (stress tests per gate and per coupling)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8062-9ffd-c93f37ee05a9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30cc5e6f-95bd-8011-bfff-f1ef2a1da97a" class="">Deliverable summary (what “All 3” produces)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30cc5e6f-95bd-80c7-b459-efd1d0f523c1" class="numbered-list"
